## Glossary

| Term | Meaning | Chapter |
|---|---|---|
| "The gate" | The plugin's validate → profile → execute → verify → ledger path | [02a](02a_history_jul28_aug31.md) |
| .builds/ | gitignored repo-local rollback/log folder | [09](09_evals_tests_dev_process.md) |
| [orion:kind] tag | Note text on events ORION created, e.g. [orion:study]; marks them movable | [06](06_scripts_life_loop.md) |
| `### EDIT` | SEARCH/REPLACE hunk edit; each SEARCH must match exactly once | [02c](02c_history_sep21_oct01.md) |
| `checkDay` | The invariant: nothing ORION places overlaps anything; blocks >= 5 minutes | [02c](02c_history_sep21_oct01.md) |
| `diffForCheckpoint` | Shows the reviewer one checkpoint's diff, not the cumulative run diff | [02c](02c_history_sep21_oct01.md) |
| `doc-report` | Check that a packet has six sections, all requirement ids and real paths | [02c](02c_history_sep21_oct01.md) |
| `entrySatisfied`, `nextPhaseAllowed` | previous phase passed; all requirements passed | [09](09_evals_tests_dev_process.md) |
| `now` verb | `orion-reschedule now` books what he is doing and slides the rest after it | [02c](02c_history_sep21_oct01.md) |
| `recoverableRuntime` | A machine failure that refunds the attempt (eviction, memory pressure, inference timeout) | [02c](02c_history_sep21_oct01.md) |
| `settleStatus` | The single function deciding what the loop can do now | [02c](02c_history_sep21_oct01.md) |
| `trusted_user` / `untrusted_external` | Input trust assigned only by `authorizeTaskGraph` | [04](04_plugin_core_actions_gate.md) |
